### [1] [LLMET: Enabling Cross-Layer Evaluation of Emerging M3D Memories for Energy-Efficient LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26491)
*Ming-Yen Lee,Hanchen Yang,Faaiq Waqar,Harsono Simka,Tushar Krishna,Muhammed Ahosan Ul Karim,Shimeng Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文开发了LLMET跨层仿真框架，系统研究了新兴存储技术（如M3D集成）扩大片上缓存对LLM服务能效的影响，发现在不同平台和模型上可实现24%-44%的芯片能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型服务的能耗问题日益严峻，片外高带宽内存（HBM）访问是主要能耗来源。新兴的M3D等技术可在逻辑芯片后端线集成更大容量片上缓存，但持续扩展片上存储能否有效提升LLM服务能效尚不清楚。

Method: 开发了LLMET验证跨层仿真框架，系统研究了不同模型、应用和平台上大容量片上存储技术的影响。利用M3D技术将L2缓存从40MB扩展至1GB，在双NVIDIA A100 GPU、8×B200类平台及边缘平台等多种配置下进行能耗仿真分析。

Result: 在双A100 GPU上，L2缓存从40MB扩展至1GB使Llama3.1-70B预填充阶段芯片能耗降低44%（16K上下文）；8×B200类平台L2缓存从128MB扩展至4GB，预填充能耗最高节省24%；边缘平台缓存从8MB扩展至256MB，解码能耗节省达30%。

Conclusion: 超大片上存储技术对构建能效优化的LLM服务系统具有重要潜力，为未来硬件设计提供了有力支持。

Abstract: The energy consumption of Large Language Model (LLM) serving is becoming a major system challenge as deployment scales, driven by hardware power and thermal constraints and rising electricity costs. A key contributor to chip energy dissipation is data movement between limited on-chip cache and off-chip High Bandwidth Memory (HBM). Meanwhile, emerging memory technologies such as monolithic 3D (M3D) integration of cache memories at the Back-End-Of-Line (BEOL) of logic chips enable larger and denser on-chip memories, creating new opportunities to reduce costly off-chip traffic. However, it remains unclear whether continuously scaling on-chip memory using emerging technologies can effectively improve the energy efficiency of LLM serving. To address this gap, we develop LLMET (LLM with Emerging Technology), a validated cross-layer simulation framework, and conduct a comprehensive study on the impact of large-capacity on-chip memory technologies across a broad range of models, applications and platforms. Utilizing M3D technology to expand the L2 cache from 40MB to 1GB yields a 44% reduction in chip energy during the Llama3.1-70B prefill phase with a 16K context window, based on LLMET simulation on a dual NVIDIA A100 GPU setup. On the 8x NVIDIA B200-like platform, extending the L2 cache from 128MB to 4GB saves the prefill energy by up to 24%. For the edge platform and workloads, the decode energy saving reaches 30% when increasing the 8MB cache size to 256MB. These results highlight the promise of ultra-large on-chip memories for energy-efficient LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [2] [NELSSA: A GPU-PNM Heterogeneous System for Mixed-Length LLM Serving via Length-based Request Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26633)
*Sookyung Choi,Seungyong Lee,Kangkyu Park,Yunseo Chun,Junseok Lee,Hyeongseok Gwak,Myunghyun Rhee,Euiseok Kim,Donguk Moon,Kwangsik Shin,Guseul Heo,Youngpyo Joo,Hoshik Kim,Jongse Park*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了NELSSA系统，通过集成GPU与近内存计算(PNM)设备来优化大语言模型在混合长度请求下的服务效率，相比纯GPU方案提升解码吞吐量最高5.5倍，降低P99延迟最高15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM服务工作负载呈现高度异构性，请求上下文长度从几百到数十万token不等，且常在同一服务窗口内交错到达。这种混合长度工作负载暴露了传统GPU中心化服务架构的根本性效率缺陷——其吞吐量依赖于大型但内存受限的批处理，难以有效应对长度差异巨大的请求混合场景。

Method: NELSSA采用异构计算架构，将GPU与近内存计算(PNM)加速器设备集成。核心技术包括：(1)基于长度的请求放置策略，将短上下文请求路由至GPU，长上下文请求路由至PNM层级；(2)运行时迁移机制，支持动态上下文增长而无需重新计算；(3)在PNM上实现设备级稀疏注意力，在GPU上实现解码内核；(4)主机端运行时系统协调调度和跨层级内存移动，基于CXL基础设施构建，支持RPC和RDMA通信。

Result: 在混合长度LLM工作负载测试中，NELSSA相比纯GPU基线系统实现：解码吞吐量提升最高5.5倍(tokens/sec)；P99延迟降低最高15倍。端到端原型系统验证了基于CXL解耦的GPU-PNM集成服务架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于CXL解耦技术实现的GPU-PNM集成服务架构是构建可扩展且灵活的LLM基础设施的有前景的系统范式，能够有效支持不断演进的工作负载需求，为未来LLM服务系统设计提供了新的技术方向。

Abstract: Modern LLMs and their agentic applications are broadening the range of serving workloads, spanning context lengths from a few hundred tokens to hundreds of thousands. As these requests frequently interleave within the same serving window, LLM serving systems must handle highly heterogeneous mixed-length workloads. Such mixed-length workloads expose fundamental inefficiencies in GPU-centric serving architectures, whose throughput depends on large, memory-constrained batches. In this paper, we present NELSSA, an LLM serving system that integrates GPUs with real-world Processing-near-Memory (PNM) accelerator devices to efficiently support mixed-length workloads. NELSSA employs length-based request placement to route short-context requests to GPUs and long-context requests to the PNM tier, incorporating runtime migration to accommodate dynamic context growth without recomputation. We prototype NELSSA as an end-to-end system, implementing device-level sparse attention on PNM, GPU decode kernels, and a host-side runtime that orchestrates scheduling and cross-tier memory movement over a CXL-enabled infrastructure with RPC and RDMA support. Across mixed-length LLM workloads, NELSSA improves decode throughput by up to 5.5x in tokens/sec and reduces P99 latency by up to 15x compared to GPU-only baselines. Our end-to-end prototype and experimental results suggest that integrated GPU-PNM serving, enabled by CXL-based disaggregation, is a promising system paradigm for scalable and flexible LLM infrastructures that support evolving workloads.

</details>


### [3] [A Low-Power Sparse Convolution Accelerator with Idle-First-Task-Assignment for Edge Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26835)
*Jingyue Zhuge,Johannes Partzsch,Christian Mayr*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向边缘视觉监控系统的低功耗稀疏卷积加速器，通过位图压缩格式、IFTA动态调度策略和专用数据流设计，在16nm工艺下实现了12-16mW的超低功耗，相比传统密集加速器在VGG16和MobileNetV2上分别获得6.5倍和2.8倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘视觉监控系统（如智慧畜牧）面临三重约束：在极有限的传输带宽和严格功耗预算下保持输入分辨率。传统密集CNN无法满足这种资源受限的IoT节点的资源限制，亟需一种低功耗、高效率的稀疏卷积加速方案。

Method: 1) 采用位图格式实现数据传输和计算的压缩，有效降低内存和带宽开销；2) 提出空闲优先任务分配(IFTA)动态调度策略，缓解稀疏计算中的负载不均衡，减少PE空闲时间并提高乘法器利用率；3) 设计专用数据流支持和加速轻量级网络中广泛使用的深度可分离卷积(DWConv)；4) 在16nm工艺下完成芯片流片和验证。

Result: 芯片核心面积仅0.5mm²，功耗低至12-16mW。在ImageNet上，针对稀疏VGG16和MobileNetV2，相比传统密集加速器分别实现6.5倍和2.8倍加速，相比现有稀疏加速器也获得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 该稀疏卷积加速器成功解决了边缘视觉监控系统的资源约束问题，在保持高计算效率的同时实现了超低功耗，为资源受限的IoT边缘设备提供了可行的CNN加速解决方案，对智能畜牧等边缘视觉应用具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: In recent years, edge-vision monitoring systems for applications such as smart animal husbandry have faced strict tripartite constraints: maintaining input resolution under extremely limited transmission bandwidth and strict power budgets. Conventional dense convolutional neural networks (CNNs) cannot satisfy the resource limits of such constrained IoT nodes. To address this challenge, this paper presents a low-power sparse convolution accelerator for edge devices, fabricated and validated in a 16 nm process. First, the accelerator adopts a bitmap-based format for compression in both data transmission and computation, effectively reducing memory and bandwidth overhead. Second, to mitigate load imbalance in sparse computation, an Idle-First-Task-Assignment (IFTA) dynamic scheduling strategy is proposed, significantly reducing processing-element (PE) idle time and improving multiplier utilization. In addition, a dedicated dataflow is designed to support and accelerate depthwise separable convolution (DWConv), which is widely used in lightweight networks. Experimental results show that the chip occupies only 0.5~mm$^2$ core area and consumes as little as 12--16~mW. On ImageNet, for sparse VGG16 and MobileNetV2, the proposed accelerator achieves 6.5$\times$ and 2.8$\times$ speedups, respectively, over traditional dense accelerators, and also delivers significant performance gains over the existing sparse accelerator.

</details>


### [4] [Investigating reservoir computing for branch predictionin pipelined processors using emerging CMOS memristor devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27140)
*Harvey Samuel George Johnson,Sendy Phang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于忆阻器的储备池计算(RC)框架用于CPU分支预测，在RISC-V架构上实现了高预测准确率，但适应速度比TAGE预测器慢15倍，需进一步优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现高速运算并与CMOS数字逻辑集成，针对多级流水线CPU核心的分支预测工作负载需求，开发新型的储备池计算实现框架。

Method: 提出基于忆阻器的储备池计算设计框架，使用System Verilog和Verilog-AMS工业标准建模语言进行仿真实现，先通过基本序列检测任务验证，再针对分支预测进行基准测试，使用Dhrystone性能基准测试并针对RISC-V RV64GC指令集架构进行评估。

Result: 开发的RC框架在分支预测应用中展现出优异的整体预测准确率，但与最先进的TAGE预测器相比，所提出的RC设计框架适应分支行为变化的速度慢15倍。

Conclusion: 储备池计算在分支预测应用中显示出巨大潜力并可实现优异的预测准确率，但需进一步改进RC设计框架以解决系统适应性问题。

Abstract: This project aimed to develop a novel reservoir compute (RC) implementation framework targeting high-speed operation and integration with CMOS digital logic. With the target workload of branch prediction (BP) for multistage pipelined central pro-cessing unit (CPU) cores. For this, a novel memristor based RC design framework was developed within the context of the workload requirements. This was then implemented in simulation using industry standard modelling languages of System Verilog (SV) and Verilog-AMS (VAMS).The developed RC design framework was subsequently verified using a basic sequence detection task before further benchmarking for its effectiveness at BP. The developed RC framework was tested using the Dhrystone performance benchmark, while targeting the RISC-V RV64GC instruction set architecture (ISA). Conducted testing demonstrates that RC shows great promise for ap-plication to BP and is capable of achieving impressive overall prediction accuracy. However, testing also shows that further refinement of the developed RC design framework is necessary to address shortfalls in the adaptability of the proposed RC system. As comparison against the state of the art TAGE predictor showed the proposed RC design framework to be 15x slower to adapt to changes in branching behaviour.

</details>

### [5] [Route-Block Membership Selects Packed-AWQ Arithmetic: A Controlled Single-Fixture Mechanism Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26316)
*Lukas Stepanek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在特定实验条件下揭示了MoE推理中路由-块预处理对计算轨迹的因果影响机制，通过干预实验证明了对齐方式、路由排列等因素能精确控制计算路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE（混合专家）推理中，将路由token对齐到填充的专家块并执行打包量化矩阵乘法的预处理过程通常被视为简单的簿记工作。作者旨在挑战这一观点，通过实验证明这些预处理操作实际上具有因果决定性影响。

Method: 在固定的Qwen3-Coder AWQ layer-6配置、vLLM/Marlin构建和RTX 3090运行时环境下，作者设计了多种路由-块干预实验：注入相反对齐、交换块内路由、跨边界交换路由等，并分析了源码和二进制层面的调度几何映射。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 两种固定的预构建历史产生不同的原生对齐和精确轨迹；(2) 注入相反对齐能转移W13、激活值、路由-W2和最终输出；(3) 跨专家块边界的路由交换能转移完整的相反轨迹；(4) 强制单切片200块网格使W13按位相等；(5) 稳定规范构造使两种历史收敛到第三条精确轨迹；(6) 确认队列包含70个有效冷进程和7次必需扰动拒绝。

Conclusion: 研究首次在特定fixture下确立了MoE路由-块预处理的因果机制，证明这些所谓的"簿记"操作实际上精确控制了打包算术轨迹。但作者明确指出这只是单个fixture的因果机制结果，不涉及普遍性、分配器、可移植性或服务影响等方面的声明。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) inference first aligns routed tokens into padded expert blocks, then executes packed quantized matrix multiplication over those blocks. This preprocessing is often treated as bookkeeping. In one pre-specified Qwen3-Coder AWQ layer-6 fixture on a pinned vLLM/Marlin build and RTX 3090 runtime, we show that the tested route-block interventions select exact packed arithmetic trajectories. Two fixed preconstruction histories produced distinct native alignments and exact trajectories. Injecting the opposite alignment transferred W13, activation, routed-W2, and final outputs. Permuting two routes within one block preserved each native trajectory, while exchanging two prior-data-selected routes across the boundary between expert-106 blocks 40 and 41 transferred the complete opposite trajectory. Source- and binary-derived schedule geometry maps those blocks to direct/full-K and split/global-reduction classes. Forcing a single-slice 200-block grid made W13 bitwise equal. Stable canonical construction made both histories converge to a third exact trajectory. The confirmatory cohort contains 70 valid cold processes and seven required perturbation rejections. This is a causal mechanism result for one fixture, not a prevalence, allocator, portability, or serving-impact claim.

</details>


### [6] [The Fabric Is the Cluster Driver: Cross-Layer eBPF Policies for GPU-CXL Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26335)
*Yiwei Yang,Andi Quinn*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: fabric_ext是一个eBPF中间件编译器和运行时，用于在GPU-CXL结构网络上实现可扩展的操作系统策略，通过语义移动图抽象实现跨设备的策略执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU-CXL结构网络的复杂性增加，需要在GPU钩子、驱动/运行时钩子、DPU/NIC钩子和CXL交换机等多设备间统一执行策略程序，传统方法难以实现跨设备的一致策略管理。LLM预填充等应用需要跨越GPU张量执行、DPU/NIC事件执行和CXL数据流岛的协同处理。

Method: 引入语义移动图作为核心抽象，边描述字节、步幅、重用距离、读写比率、源和目的地、排序要求、别名集、所有权和转换操作。编译器将图降级为每个设备的eBPF程序、验证器义务、一致性分类的BPF映射，以及bpftime和dputime的构件。在结构边缘将近类型2小核心视为硬件JIT和状态管理器，专门化验证移动描述符。

Result: fabric_ext成功实现了策略程序在GPU钩子、驱动/运行时钩子、DPU/NIC钩子和CXL交换机或近内存钩子上的统一执行，支持Move、Quantize、Compress、Checksum、Filter、Reduce、Scatter/Gather、Replicate、Persist等多种数据转换操作。

Conclusion: 通过语义移动图抽象和跨设备eBPF程序生成，fabric_ext为GPU-CXL结构网络上的可扩展操作系统策略提供了有效的解决方案，能够在LLM预填充等复杂场景下实现跨设备的数据流管理和策略执行。

Abstract: We present fabric_ext, an eBPF middleware compiler and runtime for extensible OS policies over GPU--CXL fabrics. fabric_ext lets one policy program execute across GPU hooks, driver/runtime hooks, DPU/NIC hooks, and CXL switch or near-memory hooks. The key abstraction is a semantic movement graph: edges describe bytes, stride, reuse distance, read/write ratio, source and destination, ordering requirement, alias set, ownership, and transformations such as Move, Quantize, Compress, Checksum, Filter, Reduce, Scatter/Gather, Replicate, and Persist. The compiler lowers this graph into per-device eBPF programs, verifier obligations, consistency-classed BPF maps, and artifacts for bpftime and dputime. At the fabric edge, fabric_ext treats a near-Type-2 small core as a hardware-JIT and state manager: it specializes verified movement descriptors into local copy, placement, ordering, and transformation commands, while the surrounding Von Neumann island of memory, DMA, and compute engines performs the dataflow. Because this dataflow is data-driven, fabric_ext also places observation beside the island, where queues, DMA completions, memory placement, and ownership transitions are visible as they happen. The canonical stress case is LLM prefill: attention streams KV blocks and reductions while FFN streams activations, weights, and compressible intermediates, forcing one request to cross GPU tensor execution, DPU/NIC event execution, and CXL or switch-local dataflow islands.

</details>


### [7] [StrataCL: Fabric-Native Communication Library for Production Supernodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26444)
*Tiancheng Hu,Jin Qin,Yuzheng Wang,Ke Liu,TangShengsheng Li,Sheng Wang,Zhongzhe Hu,Tianlun Hu,Wei Wang,Lijun Li,Jingbin Zhou,Xiaoming Bao,Hongwei Sun,Jieru Zhao,Huimin Cui,Tao Xie,Chenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: StrataCL是一种零冗余、原生架构的通信库，通过用户缓冲区直接通信和架构感知优化，在华为CloudMatrix384上显著提升了分布式AI工作负载的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式AI工作负载在数百个加速器上运行，通信成为主要瓶颈。现有通信库以缓冲区为中心，用户缓冲区和通信缓冲区分开管理，导致冗余数据拷贝或昂贵的用户缓冲区注册开销。

Method: 提出StrataCL通信库：1) 引入分配时注册机制实现用户缓冲区直接通信；2) 设计负载均衡的NPU核心分区通信算子；3) 采用NPU驱动的SDMA卸载技术，充分利用超节点架构特性。

Result: 在华为CloudMatrix384上：集合通信总线带宽提升最高1.6倍，MoE分发/合并总线带宽提升最高1.4倍。在生产工作负载中：LLM推理吞吐量提升1.9倍，P99 TTFT降低2.2倍，LLM和推荐系统训练迭代时间分别减少1.4倍和1.3倍。

Conclusion: StrataCL通过消除冗余拷贝和架构感知优化，有效解决了分布式AI工作负载的通信瓶颈问题，在生产环境中实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Modern distributed AI workloads run across hundreds of accelerators, making communication a major bottleneck. Existing communication libraries remain largely buffer-centric because user and communication buffers are managed separately, causing redundant data copies or costly user-buffer registration. This paper presents StrataCL, a zero-redundancy and fabric-native communication library for production supernodes. StrataCL introduces registration-on-allocation to realize user-buffer direct communication, and designs communication operators with workload-balanced NPU-core partitioning and NPU-driven SDMA offloading to exploit supernode architecture features. On the Huawei CloudMatrix384, StrataCL improves collective bus bandwidth by up to 1.6x and improves MoE dispatch/combine bus bandwidth by up to 1.4x. Across three production workloads, StrataCL improves LLM inference throughput by 1.9x, reduces P99 TTFT by 2.2x, and reduces LLM and Recsys training iteration time by 1.4x and 1.3x, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [DualDecoder: Accelerate Long Context LLM Inference by Predictive Prefetch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26475)
*Zuning Liang,Zhiyi Yao,Qi Chen,Yuedong Xu,Hao Dai,Zhiqiang Ding,Tongkai Yang,Jinlong Hou,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对长上下文LLM推理中的KV缓存内存瓶颈，提出DualDecoder系统，通过双令牌解码流水线预测关键KV条目，实现预取与计算重叠，消除GPU辅助状态开销，吞吐量提升2.62倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏KV缓存方法虽然将大部分KV条目卸载到主机内存，但在GPU中引入了大量用于检索管理的辅助状态。测量表明，这些常被忽视的辅助状态在高并发工作负载下带来显著内存开销，成为新的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出DualDecoder系统，核心包括：1）双令牌解码流水线，利用推测令牌准确预测下一令牌所需的关键KV条目；2）层感知传输调度，将KV预取与模型计算重叠；3）层作用域内存管理器减少GPU运行时缓冲区。通过预测性预取消除了GPU上的辅助状态开销。

Result: 实验结果表明，DualDecoder在保持解码延迟和模型质量的前提下，相比最先进系统将解码吞吐量提升了高达2.62倍。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙利用令牌预测性实现KV预取与计算重叠，DualDecoder成功解决了稀疏KV缓存方法中的辅助状态内存瓶颈问题，为高并发长上下文LLM推理提供了一种轻量高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference is becoming a fundamental capability for modern LLM serving, especially driven by emerging agentic applications. Yet it faces a severe memory wall that the KV cache scales proportionally with increasing context length and request concurrency. Existing sparse KV cache methods offload most KV entries to host memory and retrieve only the critical KV entries needed by each decoding step. However, they commonly introduce substantial auxiliary states in GPU memory for KV retrieval management. Our measurements show that these often-overlooked auxiliary states introduce significant memory overhead and become a new bottleneck under high-concurrency workloads.
  In this paper, we present DualDecoder, a lightweight serving system for long-context LLM inference that enables efficient sparse KV cache retrieval from host memory. Our key insight is that the critical KV entries required for decoding the next token can be accurately predicted from the preceding speculated token. This predictability enables KV retrieval to be proactively prefetched and overlapped with decoding computation, effectively eliminating the GPU memory overhead of auxiliary states. To achieve this prefetching efficiently, DualDecoder leverages a novel dual-token decoding pipeline that accurately identifies critical KV entries with negligible computational overhead, and designs a layer-aware transfer schedule to overlap KV prefetching with model computation and a layer-scoped memory manager to reduce the GPU runtime buffer. Experimental results show that DualDecoder improves decoding throughput by up to 2.62$\times$ over state-of-the-art systems while preserving decoding latency and model quality.

</details>


### [9] [Safety-Gated Autoscaling: A Multi-Layered Defense Architecture for Kubernetes Vertical Resource Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26503)
*Azra Karakaya,Erva Şengül,Ahmet Kaplan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Intelligent Cluster Optimizer，一个开源Kubernetes算子，通过包含内存泄漏检测器的五层安全流水线，在保证安全的前提下实现容器工作负载的精确资源配置，在GKE部署中实现20-40%的成本节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes资源管理困难，工程师为避免风险倾向于过度配置CPU和内存，造成资源浪费。内置的水平/垂直Pod自动扩缩容器具有滞后性，仅在被动的阈值触发后才行动，导致过度配置，甚至可能通过增加内存来掩盖软件缺陷（如内存泄漏）。现有预测性自动扩缩容器或关注预测精度或在专有基础设施上运行，异常检测仅用于告警而非阻止有害操作。

Method: 开发了Intelligent Cluster Optimizer开源Kubernetes operator，核心创新是五层安全流水线：1）基于线性回归和R²评分的内存泄漏检测器作为阻塞性关卡，如检测到泄漏则拒绝推荐；2）SLA监控；3）熔断器；4）HPA/PDB冲突检测；5）策略引擎，并提供回滚和dry-run模式。资源配置推荐通过百分位分析和Holt-Winters预测生成，采用多目标Pareto优化在容器级别进行平衡。

Result: 通过1118个自动化测试验证，代码覆盖率达80.3%。在Google Kubernetes Engine实际部署中，right-sizing产生20-40%的成本节约（what-if预测），泄漏检测关卡达到83%的检测准确率。

Conclusion: 将安全性作为一等关注点，证明了安全感知的自动扩缩容不仅可行，而且能同时降低成本和提升系统健康度。系统的开源性质和多层安全防护机制为Kubernetes资源管理提供了新的解决方案，在资源优化和安全性之间达成了良好平衡。

Abstract: Kubernetes is the standard platform for orchestrating containerized applications, yet resource management remains difficult. To stay safe, engineers over-provision CPU and memory, leaving reserved but unused capacity that is the main source of wasted cost. The built-in Horizontal and Vertical Pod Autoscalers are reactive: they act only after a threshold is crossed, which causes lag, over-provisioning, and can mask software defects by granting a leaking workload more memory. Predictive autoscalers focus on improving forecasting accuracy or run inside proprietary infrastructure, and anomaly detection is used only to alert, never to block a harmful action. The Intelligent Cluster Optimizer is an open-source Kubernetes operator that right-sizes container workloads with safety as a first-class concern. Its central contribution is a five-layer safety pipeline where a memory-leak detector, based on linear regression with R^2 scoring, acts as a blocking gate: if a leak is detected the recommendation is rejected, so the optimizer never hides a bug by enlarging a broken container. The pipeline combines SLA monitoring, a circuit breaker, HPA/PDB conflict detection, and a policy engine, with rollback and dry-run mode for human approval. Recommendations are produced by percentile analysis and Holt-Winters forecasting, balanced through multi-objective Pareto optimization at the per-container level. We validated the system with 1118 automated tests at 80.3% coverage and a live deployment on Google Kubernetes Engine, where right-sizing produced estimated cost savings of 20--40% in what-if projections and the leak gate reached 83% detection accuracy.

</details>


### [10] [ServerlessT2I: Efficient Text-to-Image Workflow Serving on a Serverless Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26566)
*Xiaoxiao Jiang,Suyi Li,Sheng Yao,Tianyu Feng,Lingyun Yang,Dapeng Nie,Haoran Yang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ServerlessT2I，一种无服务器原生系统，通过将文本到图像工作流分解为松耦合的模型函数，实现独立管理和调度，从而显著提升了资源利用效率和系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像（T2I）工作流平台采用单体式设计，将整个工作流作为不透明的GPU函数部署，导致工作流结构不透明、扩展开销大、用户需管理底层GPU协调，且在多租户集群中缺乏细粒度公平性。

Method: ServerlessT2I将T2I工作流分解为可独立管理和调度的松耦合模型函数，支持按模型扩展、声明式工作流组合、透明GPU驻留通信和公平感知调度；同时利用计算密集型T2I推理留下的空闲GPU内存构建数据平面，以减少模型加载和数据通信开销，并引入多租户公平调度器。

Result: 使用生产追踪数据评估，ServerlessT2I在相同GPU预算下可支持比现有T2I工作流服务系统高2倍的请求率；在固定请求率下，可节省多达3倍的GPU资源，同时满足服务级目标（SLOs）。

Conclusion: ServerlessT2I通过细粒度分解和独立调度，显著提升了T2I工作流在无服务器平台上的资源效率、扩展性和多租户公平性，为T2I工作流的高效服务提供了新的系统设计范式。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) workflows are increasingly deployed on serverless platforms because users often compose customized workflows and invoke them intermittently. Existing platforms typically deploy each workflow as an opaque GPU function, provisioning, placing, and scaling all constituent models in the workflow together. This monolithic design obscures workflow structure, inflates scaling overhead, forces users to manage low-level GPU coordination, and limits fine-grained fairness in multi-tenant clusters. In this paper, we present ServerlessT2I, a serverless-native system that decomposes a T2I workflow into loosely coupled model functions that can be independently managed and scheduled. By explicitly managing individual model execution, ServerlessT2I enables per-model scaling, declarative workflow composition, transparent GPU-resident communication, and fairness-aware scheduling. To make this decomposition efficient, ServerlessT2I harvests slack GPU memory left idle by compute-bound T2I inference to build a data plane that reduces model loading and data communication overheads. \sys{} further introduces a fair scheduler for multi-tenant serving. Using production traces, ServerlessT2I sustains up to 2$\times$ higher request rates than existing T2I workflow serving systems with the same GPU budget; for a fixed request rate, it saves up to 3$\times$ GPU resources while satisfying service level objectives (SLOs).

</details>


### [11] [Nix to the Rescue for a Reproducible HPC-AI Software Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26688)
*Wenke Du,Jean-Marc Gratien,Raphael Gayno,Bruno Raffin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于Nix的混合HPC/AI软件栈解决方案，在生产超级计算机环境下实现了可重复性，解决了传统方法存在的依赖发现、库泄露和组合性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC环境下的可重复性面临多重挑战：生产超级计算机存在无root权限、网络受限、软件栈跨多种语言（C/C++、Fortran、Python、MPI、GPU运行时）等约束。传统基于环境模块和Conda的方法需要手动定位依赖、会将系统库泄露到构建中、且跨项目组合性差。容器虽有助于部署但本身不保证可重复性。

Method: 采用Nix构建混合HPC/AI软件栈，实现本地工作站无root开发和生产集群上Apptainer镜像的远程部署。利用Nix的一致性包布局、完整环境隔离和基于flake的组合机制，通过单一声明式规范统一管理C/C++和Python，同时生成部署容器。使用CMake预设解决开发与生产环境的分离问题。

Result: 成功解决了依赖发现、库泄露和组合性问题，实现了C/C++和Python的统一管理。在本地无root权限的工作站和生产集群Apptainer镜像部署上都取得了良好效果。Nix的flake机制支持跨项目的环境组合。

Conclusion: Nix为HPC环境提供了有效的可重复性解决方案，相比Spack和Guix具有独特优势。但当前存在Nixpkgs中ML包覆盖不完整的问题，需要权衡不同工具的优劣并关注生态系统的发展。

Abstract: Reproducibility in HPC remains difficult under the constraints of production supercomputers: no root access, limited internet, and software stacks that increasingly span C/C++, Fortran, Python, MPI, and GPU runtimes. Traditional approaches based on environment modules and Conda require manual intervention to locate dependencies, leak system libraries into builds, and fail to compose across projects. Containers help with deployment but do not by themselves guarantee reproducibility. We report on our experience building a hybrid HPC/AI software stack with Nix, covering local development on a workstation without root and remote deployment as an Apptainer image on a production cluster. Nix's consistent package layout, full environment isolation, and flake-based composition resolve the dependency discovery, leakage, and composition problems we encountered, while unifying C/C++ and Python management under a single declarative specification that also generates the deployment container. We discuss trade-offs against Spack and Guix, the development-versus-production split addressed via CMake presets, and current gaps in ML package coverage in Nixpkgs.

</details>


### [12] [Mind the Gap: The Disconnect Between Synthetic and Natural Edge Weights in Parallel Single-Source Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26821)
*Marco D'Antonio,Thai Son Mai,Hans Vandierendonck*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究揭示现有并行SSSP算法评估中使用合成均匀权重分布与真实世界重尾分布存在显著差异，导致性能评估偏差、参数配置错误甚至性能排序颠倒，呼吁重新审视基准测试标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究中对并行单源最短路径（SSSP）算法的评估常采用合成的均匀分布边权重，但真实世界图表现出截然不同的重尾权重分布。由于SSSP实现本质上依赖权重分布进行参数调优和工作效率优化，这种评估方法与实际性能之间的脱节引发了对现有基准测试方法是否存在偏差的质疑。

Method: 1. 统计表征17个来自不同领域的真实世界图的权重分布，并与文献中使用的六种合成分布进行对比分析；\n2. 对七种最先进的并行SSSP算法进行全面评估，系统分析其对边权重的敏感性；\n3. 对比研究合成均匀权重与真实权重下算法的最优参数配置及性能层级变化。

Result: 1. 发现并行SSSP算法对边权重分布表现出严重的敏感性；\n2. 证明使用合成均匀权重评估会改变最优参数配置，并可能导致性能层级逆转；\n3. 揭示了当前基准测试标准中存在的系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 现有基准测试方法无意中偏倚了并行SSSP算法的性能结果，挑战了当前的评估标准。研究结果为严谨的SSSP算法设计提供了实用见解，强调了采用真实世界权重分布进行算法评估的重要性，并呼吁重新审视和改进算法基准测试实践。

Abstract: Scientific research works often evaluate Parallel Single-Source Shortest Path (SSSP) algorithms using synthetic, uniformly distributed edge weights. However, real-world graphs exhibit very different, often heavy-tailed, weight distributions. This creates a disconnect between how algorithms are evaluated and their real-world performance, since most SSSP implementations inherently rely on the weight distribution for parameter tuning and work efficiency. In this paper, we explore whether current benchmarking methods unintentionally bias the performance results of these algorithms. To this end, we statistically characterize the weight distributions of 17 real-world graphs from a variety of domains and contrast them with six synthetic distributions used in the literature. Through a comprehensive evaluation of seven state-of-the-art parallel SSSP algorithms, we demonstrate severe sensitivity to edge weights, and show that evaluating with synthetic uniform weights alters optimal parameter configurations and can invert the performance hierarchy. These findings challenge existing benchmarking standards and offer practical insights for rigorous SSSP algorithm design.

</details>


### [13] [Hybrid Workflow Composition for Extreme-Scale Data Processing: A Case Study on the HL-LHC (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26877)
*Alan Malta Rodrigues,Douglas Thain*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模拟框架，用于研究高吞吐量计算环境中工作流组合策略对系统性能的影响，证明了混合组合策略可显著提升吞吐量并降低网络开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高吞吐量计算（HTC）环境中，如何在工作流有向无环图（DAG）中对任务集进行战略性分组（即工作流组合）是一个关键但常被忽视的挑战。该问题直接影响执行开销和资源利用率，尤其在大规模异构资源环境下更为突出。

Method: 1. 提出了一个新颖的模拟框架，用于刻画任务集粒度与系统级约束（如作业延迟、故障率、吞吐量和I/O带宽）之间的相互作用关系；2. 通过探索高维参数空间，量化不同工作流拓扑结构的性能敏感性；3. 提出多度量目标函数，实现策略驱动的优化。

Result: 研究表明，混合组合策略（动态平衡任务集独立性与执行分组）可实现：（1）吞吐量提升高达3.8倍；（2）网络开销降低14.9倍。同时，多度量目标函数能够使系统架构师在吞吐量、I/O成本和CPU效率之间的帕累托前沿上进行权衡优化。

Conclusion: 本研究为分布式系统中的自动化工作流综合提供了严格的理论基础，为下一代科学流水线提供了可扩展的模型。所有研究资源均已公开，便于复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: High-Throughput Computing (HTC) environments tailored for high-concurrency resource efficiency require sophisticated orchestration to manage petabyte-scale data across heterogeneous resources. A critical but often overlooked challenge is workflow composition: the strategic grouping of tasksets within a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) to mitigate execution overhead while maximizing resource utilization. This paper presents a novel simulation framework for characterizing the interplay between taskset granularity and system-level constraints (e.g., job latency, failure rate, throughput, and I/O bandwidth).
  By exploring a high-dimensional parameter space, we quantify the performance sensitivity of diverse workflow topologies. Our results demonstrate that hybrid composition strategies, which dynamically balance taskset independence with execution grouping, can yield up to 3.8x throughput increase and a 14.9x reduction in network overhead. We further propose a multi-metric objective function that enables policy-driven optimization, allowing system architects to navigate the Pareto frontier between throughput, I/O cost, and CPU efficiency. These findings provide a rigorous foundation for automated workflow synthesis in distributed systems, offering a scalable model for next-generation scientific pipelines. All artifacts are publicly available.

</details>


### [14] [InferScale: GPU-Native KV Injection for Personalized LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27090)
*Peter Li,Prashant Pandey*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: InferScale是一个GPU原生LLM内存系统，通过复用KV状态替代重复的提示预填充，在检索预算增加时将TTFT降低72-79%，吞吐量提升3.7-4.5倍，同时保持应用质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产级内存系统（如Mem0、MemGPT、Zep）在每次请求时都会检索相关记忆子集并注入提示词，导致服务引擎重复预填充相同内容。随着检索预算增长，首个词元时间（TTFT）显著增加，即使底层记忆在请求间被复用。这种重复计算造成了计算资源浪费和延迟增加的问题。

Method: InferScale预计算每个记忆事实的KV表示，与语义嵌入一起存储在GPU上，服务时检索相关事实并将其KV直接注入vLLM的分页缓存。针对旋转位置嵌入下的动态记忆组装问题，提出Chunked RoPE技术，在旋转前存储键并在注入时应用服务时位置。为缓解独立编码记忆事实时缺失的跨事实上下文问题，提出Context-Window Encoding，将每个记忆事实与少量前驱对话上下文一起编码，但仅缓存目标事实的KV。系统通过vLLM的KV-connector接口实现，无需引擎修改或模型微调。

Result: 在LoCoMo数据集上跨三个开放权重模型进行评估：当k=50时，InferScale将TTFT降低72-79%（3.6-4.8倍加速），准确率达到60.3%（相比Mem0的63.3%，无需服务时重计算），并在并发负载下实现3.7-4.5倍吞吐量提升。TTFT在检索预算增加时几乎保持恒定。

Conclusion: 可复用KV状态成功将记忆条件服务延迟与检索上下文大小解耦，同时保持应用质量。InferScale为生产环境中的个性化LLM服务提供了一种高效的解决方案，证明了KV状态复用范式在处理持久化个人上下文场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed with persistent personalized context, such as accumulated memory profiles or long conversation histories, that is shared across a user's many requests. Production memory systems (e.g., Mem0, MemGPT, and Zep) retrieve a relevant subset of this memory and inject it into the prompt, forcing the serving engine to repeatedly prefill the same content. As the retrieval budget grows, time-to-first-token (TTFT) increases even though the underlying memory is reused across requests.
  We present InferScale, a GPU-native LLM memory system that replaces repeated prompt prefilling with reusable KV state. InferScale precomputes each memory fact's KV representation, stores it alongside a semantic embedding on the GPU, retrieves relevant facts at serving time, and injects their KV directly into vLLM's paged cache. To support dynamically assembled memories under rotary position embeddings, we introduce Chunked RoPE, which stores keys before rotation and applies their serving-time positions during injection. However, encoding memory facts independently omits the cross-fact context available during joint prefilling. We mitigate this with Context-Window Encoding, which encodes each memory fact together with a small window of preceding conversation context while caching only the target fact's KV.
  InferScale is implemented through vLLM's KV-connector interface, requiring neither engine modifications nor model fine-tuning. Across three open-weight models on LoCoMo, InferScale keeps TTFT nearly constant as the retrieval budget increases: at k=50 it reduces TTFT by 72-79% (3.6-4.8x), achieves 60.3% accuracy versus 63.3% for Mem0 without serving-time recomputation, and delivers 3.7-4.5x the throughput under concurrent load. Reusable KV state thus decouples memory-conditioned serving latency from retrieved-context size while preserving application quality.

</details>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [15] [A Persona-based Rate Action Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26545)
*Hayden Helm,Andrew Dassori*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于数字角色的FOMC利率决策预测指数，通过构建25,000个文本块的数据集并使用生成式系统，成功预测利率周期并领先目标利率约三个季度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测美国联邦公开市场委员会(FOMC)的利率决策（加息/维持/降息）对于金融市场和货币政策研究具有重要意义。传统方法难以捕捉决策者随时间变化的行为模式和立场变化，因此需要一种能够动态建模和预测FOMC成员集体行为的新方法。

Method: 研究者构建了包含近25,000个可检索文本块的新数据集，将数据按成员划分为独立语料库，并为每位成员创建一个生成式"角色"(persona)。通过评估角色的可识别性和可检测性来验证系统有效性，并使用查询条件化的角色表示来捕捉成员的货币政策立场，最终构建基于角色的利率行动指数。

Result: 实验结果显示：角色行为具有高度可归因性（成员条件召回率是随机水平的8倍），生成内容与真实内容难以区分(τ_det=0.23)。角色表示成功捕捉了成员的鹰派-鸽派立场排序(Kendall's τ=0.63, p<0.001)。在2022-2025年间，指数有效追踪利率周期(Kendall's τ=0.68)，构建的分类器预测准确率达0.69（基线0.47），且指数领先联邦基金目标利率约三个季度。

Conclusion: 本研究首次证明了通过数字角色集合捕捉时变群体行为的可行性，为货币政策预测提供了新的方法论框架。所提出的基于角色的指数不仅能够有效预测FOMC利率决策，还具有重要的实际应用价值，为金融市场参与者和政策研究者提供了有价值的预测工具。

Abstract: We propose an index for predicting the U.S.\ Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) decision to hike/hold/cut the current federal funds target rate based on how a collection of personas responds to current market conditions. To construct the index, we collected a new dataset consisting of nearly $25{,}000$ retrievable chunks from publicly available data. We partition the data into per-member corpora and use each as the retrieval database of a generative system we refer to throughout as a ``persona''. We first evaluate the personas across two complementary components of likeness: identifiability and detectability. Each persona's behavior is highly attributable (average member-conditional recall is $ 8\times $ chance) and generated content is nearly indistinguishable from held-out real content ($\hatτ_{\mathrm{det}} = 0.23$ against a $0.15$ floor). We then present evidence that query-conditioned representations of the personas capture members' monetary-policy stance relative to a known hawk--dove reputational ordering (Kendall's $τ= 0.63$, $p < 0.001$), substantially outperforming retrieval-only representations. These representations vary with time and current market conditions and form the basis of our proposed persona-based rate action index. For the $2022$--$2025$ period the index tracks the rate cycle (Kendall's $τ= 0.68$, $p < 10^{-6}$) and can be used to construct a simple classifier that predicts per-meeting outcomes at non-trivial accuracy ($0.69$ versus a $0.47$ base rate). Importantly, the index outperforms informative baselines and leads the federal funds target rate by roughly three quarters. As far as we are aware, our results are the first to demonstrate the ability to capture time-varying group behavior via a collection of digital personas.

</details>

*Jingjie Ning,Shanshan Zhong,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Automated research systems use experimental scores both to deliver artifacts and to decide which ideas to retain, transfer, and pursue. Yet one run scores one implementation of an idea. Crediting that realization-level score as evidence about the parent mechanism creates the \emph{implementation lottery}, in which an idea-level conclusion depends on which plausible implementation was sampled. The mismatch is structural whenever one run updates beliefs about a mechanism. We estimate its magnitude. The \emph{Idea Reliability Audit} measures \emph{idea reliability} by validating and freezing candidate cards, sampling fresh-session implementations, using outcome-blind fidelity labels, and rerunning saved artifacts. It reports idea ICC and leave-one-implementation-out (LOO) winner reversal. Prior work generally repeats the task; we repeat the idea. Across 312 assignments on 13 tabular tasks and two coding-agent setups, implementation variance was more than five and ten times same-artifact rerun variance, respectively, and the winner from one implementation draw differed from the winner under the other-two mean in 25.6\% and 43.6\% of decisions. Reversal survives card-level filtering under two outcome-blind review rules. An exploratory diagnostic on three materials-regression workflows with a deterministic evaluator also finds implementation variation dominating the decomposition. These findings distinguish idea reliability from best-of-$N$ artifact utility. Before a score guides idea-level branching, transfer, or research memory, evidence should cover multiple implementations.

</details>


### [17] [Living-Harness Is an Interactive-Agent Evolver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26598)
*Yuetian Du,Yucheng Wang,He Xu,Jiexu Xu,Shanwen Tan,Bing Zhao,Boyu Yang,Zhijie Xu,Ming Kong,Hu Wei,Jie Liu,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Living-Harness，一种自演化的智能体框架，能将执行轨迹转化为后验证据以持续更新程序性知识，在多个交互环境中显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体虽然能在单次任务中从失败中恢复，但相同的执行失败往往在后续任务中重复出现，因为任务后的反馈很少修正指导未来交互的持久化框架。静态框架通过固定的工具、上下文、记忆和工作流结构提高了可靠性，但在部署后保持不变，无法积累经验。

Method: 提出Living-Harness自演化智能体框架，包含：(1) Evolution-SOP（标准操作流程）指导的领域级演化机制；(2) 将完成轨迹和评估信号转化为后验证据进行有界框架更新；(3) 提取片段抽象和结构化更新证据；(4) 写入两种互补的程序性知识：记录触发条件、失败模式和恢复动作的情景记忆，以及记录状态节点、修复边和转换规则的状态图；(5) 保持工具和基础上下文冻结，仅更新框架状态以累积过程性修复。

Result: 在来自τ²-Bench和MultiWOZ-2.4的八个交互环境中，Living-Harness相比最强交互基线分别提升了10.07和9.91个百分点的平均Pass@1，并支持跨模型骨干进行检索式重用演化后的框架状态。

Conclusion: Living-Harness成功实现了智能体框架的自演化能力，通过将执行经验转化为可重用的程序性知识，显著提升了跨任务的性能表现，证明了过程性知识积累的有效性，并展现了良好的跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents may recover from a failure within an episode or after a retry, yet the same execution failure can recur in later tasks because post-episode feedback rarely revises the persistent harness that guides future interactions. Static harnesses improve reliability through fixed tools, context, memory, and workflow structures, but remain unchanged after deployment. We propose $\textbf{Living-Harness}$, a self-evolving agent harness that converts each completed trajectory and its evaluator signals into posterior evidence for bounded harness updates. Guided by a domain-level $\textbf{Evolution-SOP}$ ($\textbf{S}$tandard $\textbf{O}$perating $\textbf{P}$rocedure), Living-Harness extracts an episode abstraction and structured update evidence, and writes two complementary forms of procedural knowledge: episodic memory that records trigger conditions, failure patterns, and recovery actions, and a state graph that records state nodes, repair edges, and transition rules. The updated harness state is retrieved to guide future interactions, while tools and base context remain frozen, allowing procedural repairs to accumulate across evolution cycles. On eight interactive environments derived from $τ^2$-Bench and MultiWOZ-2.4, Living-Harness improves average Pass@1 over the strongest interactive baseline by 10.07 and 9.91 percentage points, respectively, and supports retrieval-only reuse of the evolved harness state across model backbones.

</details>


### [18] [DREvo: Distilling Recalibrated Historical Experience for Harness Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26722)
*Hanghui Guo,Weijie Shi,Zhangze Chen,Shengxiang Xu,Yishu Wang,Yimei Zhang,Wangze Ni,Jia Zhu,Shimin Di*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对大模型智能体Harness自演化中历史经验利用不足的问题，提出DREvo方法，通过动态验证历史经验有效性和明确搜索方向，在有限演化预算下实现了更稳定的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Harness自演化方法在利用历史经验时存在两个关键局限：1）缺乏对历史经验在当前Harness下是否有效的动态重评估；2）缺乏将有效历史经验转化为可操作搜索方向的显式机制。这导致演化轨迹波动大，难以在有限预算内稳定地发现高性能Harness。

Method: 提出DREvo方法，集成三个核心组件：函数级证据锚定、状态依赖的证据重校准、角色条件的搜索意图蒸馏。这些机制共同决定哪些历史证据仍然有效，以及Harness下一步应该向哪个方向演化。

Result: 在五个基准测试的全部任务上，DREvo均达到最高准确率；在领域推理任务和智能体任务上，相比基线方法平均提升16.2%和14.2%；演化轨迹更平滑稳定。

Conclusion: 通过动态验证历史经验有效性并将其转化为明确的搜索方向，DREvo在有限演化预算下实现了更稳定、更高效的Harness自演化，显著提升了大模型智能体的性能。

Abstract: Harness plays a critical role in large language model agent performance, and building a high-performing harness requires substantial expert effort. Therefore, recent research has increasingly explored harness self-evolution, which iteratively proposes, evaluates, and improves harnesses using historical trial experience. However, accumulated historical experience does not always translate into stable search guidance, and performance often fluctuates substantially across evolution iterations, making it difficult to reliably discover high-performing harnesses under a limited evolution budget. We identify two limitations in how existing harness self-evolution methods leverage historical experience: (1) Lack of dynamic reassessment of whether historical experience remains valid for the current harness, and (2) Lack of explicit mechanisms for translating valid historical experience into actionable search directions. To address these limitations, we propose a new harness self-evolution method, named DREvo, which integrates function-level evidence anchoring, state-dependent evidence recalibration, and role-conditioned search intent distillation to determine which historical evidence remains valid and where the harness should evolve next. Under limited evolution budgets, DREvo exhibits smoother evolution trajectories, achieves the highest accuracy on all five benchmarks, and delivers average gains of 16.2% and 14.2% over the evaluated baselines on domain reasoning and agentic tasks, respectively.

</details>


### [19] [Cross-organisational Process Mining from Message Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26917)
*Pieter Kwantes,Jetty Kleijn*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从消息日志（而非事件日志）合成行业网（Industry nets），通过将消息日志转换为保持因果顺序的事件日志来解决跨组织数据获取困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在之前的贡献中，已经建立了从事件日志发现单一过程（建模为企业网）并合成异步通信过程系统（建模为行业网）的方法。然而，在实践中，从不同组织获取事件日志可能很困难，甚至无法访问。相比之下，过程之间的消息交换记录可能更容易获得。因此，本文转向研究消息日志，即以消息交换形式（输出和输入事件）记录两个过程之间通信的集合。

Method: 本文提出了一种将消息日志转换为事件日志的方法，使得消息事件之间的因果顺序得以保持。具体而言，首先从消息日志中提取通信行为描述，然后构建事件日志以保留消息事件的因果顺序。最后，利用之前贡献中提出的方法，使用转换后的事件日志来合成行业网。

Result: 成功开发了一种将消息日志转换为事件日志的方法，该方法能够保持消息事件之间的因果顺序。转换后的事件日志可以用于合成行业网，从而实现从消息日志到行业网模型的完整流程。

Conclusion: 本文解决了从消息日志合成行业网的问题，为实际应用中难以获取跨组织事件日志的情况提供了可行的替代方案。通过保持消息事件的因果顺序，确保了合成模型的正确性和可靠性，扩展了企业网和行业网框架的应用范围。

Abstract: Enterprise nets and Industry nets are specific Petri net models that together form a framework for the modelling of collaborating business processes and the specification of interactions between them. In this set-up, individual business process are modelled as Enterprise nets, while Industry nets consist of Enterprise nets that interact by exchanging messages through unidirectional channels. In previous contributions (see [21,22]), it was established how to leverage algorithms for the discovery of single processes (modelled as Enterprise nets) from event logs (consisting of finite sequences of executed actions), to synthesise systems consisting of asynchronously communicating processes (modelled as Industry nets). However, in general, it may be difficult to work with event logs from different organisations or even get access to them. Recordings of message exchanges between processes may be easier available.Therefore, we focus in this paper on the communications between different processes and turn to the investigation of message logs, \ie collections of recordings of communications in the form of message exchanges (output and input events) between two processes. In particular, the question is addressed of how a message log can be transformed into a description of the communication behaviour in the form of an event log such that the causal order on message events is preserved. Following the approach from [21,22], this event log can then be used to synthesis an Industry net.

</details>

### [20] [ProFlow: RL-Driven and Performance-Aware Proactive Flow Placement in Datacenter Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26231)
*Sourya Saha,Md Nurul Absur,Saptarshi Debroy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出ProFlow框架，利用早期拥塞信号和强化学习在吞吐量下降前主动重路由关键流量，在FABRIC测试平台上实现了约40%的平均吞吐量提升，并提前约34秒启动重路由决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由叶交换机和汇聚交换机组成的数据中心网络中，竞争流量可能在共享的汇聚交换机上并发，导致拥塞并严重降低受保护流的性能。然而，现有拥塞管理方法主要在拥塞变得可见后才做出反应，未能利用吞吐量下降之前的早期征兆（如流量活动增加和队列溢出信号），导致拥塞管理存在滞后性。

Method: 提出ProFlow——一种主动的流量放置框架。该方法利用分布式遥测信号获取网络状态，结合离线训练的强化学习（RL）模型识别前兆性拥塞条件，在吞吐量下降发生前主动地对受保护流量进行重路由，从而实现对潜在性能下降的预测性管理。

Result: 在FABRIC测试平台上的评估表明：与反应式重路由基线相比，ProFlow实现了约40%的更高平均吞吐量，并且平均提前约34秒启动重路由决策，验证了前瞻性拥塞管理的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过充分利用吞吐量下降的早期信号并采用强化学习进行预测性决策，ProFlow证明了主动式拥塞管理能够显著提升关键流量的性能表现，为多租户数据中心网络的流量保护提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: In datacenter fabrics composed of leaf and aggregation switches, competing flows may become co-located on shared aggregation switches, creating congestion that can significantly degrade protected flows. However, before throughput degradation becomes observable, the network often exhibits early signs characterized by rising flow activity and queue overflow signals. Existing congestion-management approaches primarily react only after congestion becomes visible, leaving these early signs largely unexploited. In this paper, we propose ProFlow, a proactive flow-placement framework for protecting performance-sensitive traffic in multi-tenant datacenter networks, thereby utilizing the early signs of potential throughput degradations. ProFlow leverages distributed telemetry signals and offline-trained reinforcement learning (RL) to identify precursor congestion conditions and proactively reroute protected flows before throughput degradation occurs. Evaluation results using FABRIC testbed show that ProFlow achieves approximately 40% higher mean throughput than a reactive rerouting baseline while initiating rerouting decisions around 34 seconds earlier on average, demonstrating the effectiveness of anticipatory congestion management.

</details>


### [21] [Round Trip Time: A Benign Signal or an Indirect Window into Datacenter Workloads?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26239)
*Sourya Saha,Md Nurul Absur,Saptarshi Debroy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多租户数据中心网络中存在一种基于共享拥塞的侧信道漏洞，攻击者可通过观测RTT延迟变化推断其他租户的工作负载信息，准确率高达97.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多租户数据中心网络越来越多地依赖共享的leaf-spine架构，多个租户的流量通过公共网络资源传输。虽然逻辑隔离机制可以防止租户之间的直接访问，但共享拥塞动态仍可能通过可观察的延迟变化暴露关于共置工作负载的间接信息。现有研究缺乏对这种通过共享拥塞行为引发的侧信道漏洞的深入理解。

Method: 研究者开发了一个系统框架，用于探索工作负载引起的延迟变化是否包含足够可区分的特征，从而在现实部署条件下实现工作负载推断。该框架利用在重叠网络路径上收集的RTT（往返时延）观测数据，分析共享拥塞行为如何泄露工作负载信息。

Result: 评估结果表明，间接RTT观测可以揭示有意义的工作负载信息。在交叉路径评估场景下，当工作负载引起的拥塞足够可观察时，该方法在运行级别（run-level）上实现了高达97.3%的准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在现代数据中心基础设施中，仅靠逻辑网络隔离可能不足以防止通过共享拥塞动态产生的信息泄露。这揭示了当前多租户数据中心安全架构的一个重要隐患，需要引起重视。

Abstract: Multi-tenant datacenter networks increasingly rely on shared leaf-spine fabrics, where traffic from multiple tenants traverses common network resources. While logical isolation mechanisms prevent direct access between tenants, shared congestion dynamics may still expose indirect information about co-located workloads through observable latency variations. In this paper, we investigate a network side-channel vulnerability arising from shared congestion behavior in multi-tenant datacenter fabrics using RTT observations collected along overlapping network paths. We develop a framework to explore how workload-induced latency variations contain sufficiently distinguishable signatures to enable workload inference under realistic deployment conditions. Our evaluations show that indirect RTT observations can reveal meaningful workload information, achieving up to 97.3\% run-level accuracy under cross-path evaluation when workload-induced congestion is sufficiently observable. The findings suggest that logical network isolation alone may be insufficient to prevent information leakage through shared congestion dynamics in modern datacenter infrastructures.

</details>


### [22] [Incast-Free MoE Rate-Based Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26340)
*Evyatar Cohen,Jose Yallouz,Alexander Shpiner,Mark Silberstein,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Isaac Keslassy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文发现了混合专家模型中的指数级incast现象，并提出了一种主动公平调度框架来解决这个问题，显著提升了网络性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合专家模型架构中采用的轮询调度机制会引发严重的网络瓶颈，导致此前未被发现的指数级incast现象，影响整体系统性能。

Method: 提出了一种专为MoE工作负载定制的主动公平调度框架，通过预防性机制避免网络结构过载，并探讨了该框架在网络接口卡(NIC)中的实现方案。

Result: 通过真实和合成工作负载的大量仿真实验表明，该框架能够持续消除incast现象，保持接近100%的链路利用率，并显著降低集合完成时间(CCT)。

Conclusion: 针对MoE架构设计的主动公平调度框架能够有效解决轮询调度带来的性能瓶颈问题，为大规模语言模型的网络优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture of Experts (MoE) architectures have become key to large language models; however, their typical round-robin (RR) scheduling introduces significant bottlenecks.
  In this paper, we demonstrate that RR causes a previously-undiscovered exponential incast phenomenon with MoE traffic. We propose an alternative proactive fair scheduling framework tailored for MoE workloads, which effectively prevents fabric oversubscription. We also outline how it can be implemented in NICs. Finally, through extensive simulations with real and synthetic workloads, we demonstrate that this framework consistently eliminates incast, maintains a near-100% link utilization, and reduces Collective Completion Time (CCT).

</details>


### [23] [Pramana: A Composable, Domain-Specific Backend for Empirical Networking Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26352)
*Jaber Daneshamooz,Eugene Vuong,Alagappan Ramanathan,Manni Moghimi,Haarika Manda,Satyam Kumar,Snithik Thode,Satyandra Guthula,Sylee Beltiukov,Dongsu Han,Tarun Mangla,Sangeetha Abdu Jyothi,Walter Willinger,Arpit Gupta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pramana系统，旨在通过统一的意图规范缩小网络研究从构想到数据生成的差距，实验表明其意图规范覆盖了来自66篇论文的255个意图，概念验证实现满足率达34%，远超现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络研究中，将假设转化为实证证据的过程耗时费力，导致构想与数据生成之间存在滞后；随着AI辅助构想的加速，缺乏高效的数据生成后端成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出Pramana，一个可组合的领域特定后端，采用单一致意图规范将实验分解为意图（生成什么数据）、底物（在哪里生成）和机制（如何生成）三个独立轴，使同一规范可在任意底物上运行。

Result: 构建了涵盖66篇已发表论文的255个数据生成意图语料库，Pramana意图规范满足所有意图，现有工具最多仅满足13%；概念验证实现满足34%，为现有最佳工具的两倍以上。

Conclusion: Pramana通过统一的意图规范显著缩小了抽象与实现之间的差距，展示了其加速实证网络研究的潜力，并提出通过社区合作完善系统的路线图。

Abstract: Networking research advances by turning hypotheses into empirical evidence, so accelerating it means reducing the lag between ideation (synthesizing a hypothesis) and generating the data that tests it. Consider a concrete case: does a bulk BBR download fairly share its bottleneck with competing real-time Google Meet traffic? Validating this requires configuring a realistic bottleneck link, concurrently generating BBR's bulk transfer and Meet's real-time traffic, and collecting relevant service-quality metrics. Today this overhead is high, often forcing researchers to start from scratch for every new idea. This ideation-to-data-generation gap will only worsen in the agentic AI era, where AI-assisted ideation accelerates exponentially, yet its outputs cannot be validated without a data-generation backend.
  This paper explores how to bridge this gap. We envision a composable, domain-specific backend, Pramana, shaped as a thin waist, with diverse research intents at the top and disparate execution substrates at the bottom. Pramana realizes this waist through a single contract, the intent specification, which disaggregates an experiment into three independent axes: the intent (what data to generate), the substrate (where to generate it), and the mechanism (how to produce it), so one specification runs on any substrate. We demonstrate Pramana's utility by building a first-of-its-kind corpus of 255 data-generation intents mined from 66 published papers, and show the intent specification satisfies all of them, where no existing tool satisfies more than 13%. Our current proof-of-concept implementation already satisfies 34% of these intents, more than twice the best existing tool, and we lay out a roadmap for closing this abstraction-implementation gap through a broader community effort to build the envisioned data-generation backend and accelerate empirical networking research.

</details>

*Genze Jiang,Yizhou Huang,Kezhi Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机械审计方法，通过检查AI函数的内部表示并与可机器验证的3GPP规范进行对比，解决了无线网络中AI决策缺乏透明度的问题，并提出了审计原生网络架构以支持AI功能的持续验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络运营商越来越多地探索使用人工智能（AI）自动化复杂的网络任务，如小区选择和移动性管理。然而，目前无法验证AI功能是否基于正确的原因做出正确的决策，这种缺乏透明度的现象在对安全性和韧性要求极高的基础设施中构成了重大挑战，阻碍了AI技术在无线网络中的广泛应用。

Method: 提出了一种机械审计方法，包括三步审计原则：定位协议相关特征、验证其因果作用、诊断适应如何重塑其使用。该方法通过检查函数的内部表示并与可机器验证的3GPP规范进行对比来实现验证。同时设计了审计原生网络架构，其中包含专用的验证代理，持续检查网络中AI功能的推理过程。

Result: 提出了一个支持预部署认证和运行时审计的审计原生网络架构，该架构中的专用验证代理能够持续检查网络中AI功能的推理过程，为AI决策提供了透明度和可验证性。

Conclusion: 本文为AI在电信网络中的可信部署提供了理论基础和实践路径，但机械审计在进入电信实践和标准化之前仍面临数据、基准测试和开放性挑战等有待解决的问题。

Abstract: Mobile network operators are increasingly exploring the use of artificial intelligence (AI) to automate complex network tasks, such as cell selection and mobility management. A fundamental problem arises: there is currently no way to verify that an AI function is making the right decisions or for the right reasons, rather than arriving at correct-looking answers through unreliable shortcuts. In safety-critical and resilience-focused infrastructure, this lack of transparency poses a significant challenge to the widespread adoption of AI technologies in wireless networks. In this paper, we propose a mechanical auditing approach: inspecting a function's internal representations and checking them against machine-verifiable 3GPP specifications. Specifically, we set out a general three-step auditing principle that locates protocol-relevant features, verifies their causal role, and diagnoses how adaptation reshapes their use, grounding it throughout publicly available interpretability and telecommunications research. We present an audit-native network architecture in which a dedicated verification agent continuously checks the reasoning of AI functions in networks, supporting both predeployment certification and runtime auditing. We also discuss how it could be realised, the data and benchmarks, as well as the open challenges that remain before mechanistic auditing can enter telecommunications practice and standardisation.

</details>

Result: 实现了卓越的三维波束控制能力，俯仰角扫描范围为-87°至+87°，方位角实现360°全覆盖；天线效率在68%至80%之间，仅有10%的性能衰减。

Conclusion: 基于动态可调石墨烯超表面的半球形透射阵列天线成功实现了太赫兹频段的宽角电子波束控制，证明了石墨烯材料在实现高性能可重构天线方面的巨大潜力，为太赫兹通信和成像系统提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: This work expands the application of dynamically tunable graphene to implement a hemispherical transmitarray antenna tailored for wide-angle electronic beam steering and ultrafast moving-target tracking at 250 GHz. The proposed hemispherical transmitarray antenna features a dynamically reconfigurable graphene-based multilayer configuration consisting of gold radiating patches, biased graphene sectors, hBN dielectric layers, and a centrally positioned hornfeed. The analytical framework of graphene-based reconfigurable metasurfaces including graphene surface-conductivity modeling, voltage-controlled surface impedance, transmission-coefficient, and conformal array-factor analysis, has been established for hemispherical transmitarray antenna for the first time. Moreover, the analytical and practical mapping between desired beam directions and the corresponding graphene bias voltages have been developed for the transmitarray antenna for the first time. Our transmitarray antenna possesses exceptional 3D beamsteering, providing wide-angle elevation scanning from -87 degrees to 87 degrees and full 360-degree azimuthal coverage. It also exhibits an antenna efficiency ranging from 68% to 80% with a 10% degradation.

Abstract: The rapid development of the Internet of Everything (IoE) is accelerating the adoption of intelligent applications. However, the massive number of connected devices generates diverse and heterogeneous tasks, which pose increasing challenges for dynamic resource scheduling in IoE environments. Using their superior semantic understanding and reasoning capabilities, Large Artificial Intelligence Models (LAIMs) demonstrate significant potential to handle complex scheduling scenarios and improve resource utilization efficiency. This paper investigates a task-oriented LAIM-driven resource scheduling mechanism, which constructs a multidimensional scheduling decision model by integrating task semantics, network states, and constraint conditions. Furthermore, a task-oriented prompt generation method is designed to establish a deep association between task requirements and network state. In the proposed resource allocation scheme, an external evaluation and feedback module is incorporated to conduct real-time feasibility verification and performance evaluation of scheduling strategies, thus enhancing the robustness and adaptability of scheduling. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed Large Language Model (LLM)-driven network architecture and resource allocation scheme achieve significant improvements in convergence speed, processing latency, and energy consumption, effectively enhancing IoE task responsiveness and resource utilization.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出了一种新颖的行可移动有源可重构智能表面（RM-A-RIS）辅助车载语义通信架构，将主动信号放大与元素移动性相结合；制定了联合优化问题以最大化语义频谱效率（SSE），协调RIS元素位置、有源反射系数和语义符号长度；开发了高效的交替优化（AO）算法来解决耦合的非凸问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案显著优于现有基准方法，在总语义频谱效率（Sum-SSE）方面，相比被动RIS、固定位置有源RIS和QPSO基准分别实现了132.9%、9.2%和35.2%的提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的RM-A-RIS辅助车载语义通信系统通过有源放大和元素移动性的独特组合，有效解决了车载环境中的信号阻塞和快速时变信道问题，为可靠的车载语义通信提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Severe signal blockage and fast-varying channels in vehicular environments pose critical challenges to reliable semantic communication. To address these, this paper proposes a novel Row-Movable Active Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface (RM-A-RIS) assisted vehicular semantic communication system. This architecture uniquely combines active signal amplification with element mobility to compensate for multiplicative fading and reconstruct channel geometry, thereby enhancing spatial diversity. We formulate a joint optimization problem to maximize Semantic Spectral Efficiency (SSE) by coordinating RIS element positions, active reflection coefficients, and semantic symbol length. An efficient Alternating Optimization (AO) algorithm is developed to tackle the coupled non-convexity. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme substantially outperforms existing benchmarks, achieving up to 132.9%, 9.2%, and 35.2% improvements in Sum-Semantic Spectral Efficiency (Sum-SSE) compared to the passive RIS, fixed-position active RIS, and QPSO baselines, respectively.

</details>


### [28] [The Price of Meaning: Quantifying Semantic Communication Overheads in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26764)
*Xinyi Lin,Peizheng Li,Adnan Aijaz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对语义通信建立了开销感知分析框架，推导了语义通信在频谱和能耗方面产生效益的盈亏平衡条件，发现语义通信仅在足够大的数据包下才有益，且多用户下行场景更易获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信虽然承诺通过传递任务相关的语义而非原始比特来减少传输负载，但在实际应用中会引入语义元数据、控制信令、反馈、模型/知识库同步以及神经计算开销，这些开销可能会抵消语义压缩带来的增益，因此需要量化分析语义通信的真实成本效益。

Method: 本文提出了一个开销感知的分析框架，在保证任务效用相等的前提下量化语义通信的频谱资源和能量成本。该框架覆盖了点对点传输、UE到gNB上行链路以及单gNB下的UE到UE通信场景，并推导了关于数据包大小、语义压缩因子、模型重用、协议开销和计算能耗的闭式盈亏平衡条件。

Result: 仿真结果表明，语义通信仅在足够大的数据包下才具有频谱优势；由于处理和同步开销，能量增益需要更大的数据包才能实现；多用户下行场景特别有利于语义通信，因为共享的语义开销可以分摊到多个用户设备上。

Conclusion: 研究结果为语义通信的实际评估和面向标准化的部署提供了设计指导，强调了在实际应用中需要综合考虑各种开销因素，并为语义通信的适用场景提供了明确的界限条件。

Abstract: Semantic communication (SemCom) promises to reduce transmitted payloads by conveying task-relevant meaning instead of raw bits. However, practical SemCom also incurs semantic metadata, control signaling, feedback, model or knowledge-base synchronization, and neural computation costs, which may offset semantic compression gains. This paper develops an overhead-aware analytical framework for quantifying the spectral-resource and energy costs of SemCom under equal task utility. The framework covers point-to-point transmission, user equipment (UE)-to-next-generation NodeB (gNB) uplink, and UE-to-UE communication under a single gNB, and derives closed-form break-even conditions with respect to payload size, semantic compression factor, model reuse, protocol overhead, and computation energy. Simulation results show that SemCom becomes spectrally beneficial only for sufficiently large payloads, while energy gains require larger payloads due to processing and synchronization overheads. The results also show that multi-user downlink is particularly favorable, as shared semantic overheads can be amortized across multiple UEs. These findings provide design guidance for realistic SemCom evaluation and standardization-oriented deployment.

</details>


### [29] [Assurance-Scoped Reliability for Agentic Networks: Capturing the State That Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26953)
*Bilgehan Erman,Andrea Francini,Nikos Papadis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了可靠保障智能（RAI）架构，用于解决代理网络在将意图转化为运营服务过程中产生的传统可靠性指标无法捕捉的失效模式，通过服务可靠性档案和上下文胶囊实现检测、解释、恢复和审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理网络具备自主推理、自适应规划、工具调用和跨域协调等能力，但这些能力引入了传统可靠性度量无法完全捕获的失效模式，例如基于过期信息执行、重复外部操作、仅应用部分变更或进入放宽策略执行的回退模式。这些失效可能导致服务看似正常运行但实际行为不安全或不可问责，且缺乏足够证据进行检测、解释或恢复。

Method: 提出可靠保障智能（RAI）通用保障架构，包含两个核心组件：（1）从服务描述推导服务可靠性档案，明确需要检查、记录、恢复和审计的内容；（2）运行时通过通用函数利用服务档案，在适应服务条件的上下文胶囊中保存恢复和问责所需的持久状态。以确定性网络服务的代理生命周期管理器作为实例进行架构设计，并提出可靠性保障验证方法。

Result: 成功设计了RAI架构，通过服务可靠性档案实现可靠性需求的系统化定义，通过上下文胶囊机制确保持久状态的保存与恢复能力，为代理网络系统提供了检测、解释、恢复和审计失效的能力，并建立了相应的验证方法论。

Conclusion: RAI架构为代理网络系统提供了系统化的可靠性保障框架，能够有效捕获和应对传统可靠性度量无法覆盖的失效模式，通过服务档案和上下文胶囊的结合，实现了对不安全或不可问责行为的检测、解释、恢复和审计能力，为代理网络的可靠性保障提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Agentic networks transform accepted intents into operational services through autonomous reasoning, adaptive planning, tool use, and cross-domain coordination, but these capabilities introduce failure modes that conventional reliability measures do not fully capture. An accepted intent may still be carried out incorrectly, for example because the system acts on stale information, repeats an external action, applies only part of a change, or enters a fallback mode that quietly relaxes policy enforcement. Such failures can leave a service running and apparently healthy while its behavior is unsafe or unaccountable, with too little evidence to detect, explain, or recover from them. This article proposes Reliability Assurance Intelligence (RAI), a general assurance architecture for such systems. From the service description, RAI derives a per-service reliability profile that states what must be checked, recorded, recovered, and audited. At runtime, generic functions use the service profile to retain the durable state needed for recovery and accountability in a context capsule adapted to service conditions. Using an agentic lifecycle manager for deterministic network services as a running example, we design the RAI architecture and propose a methodology for validating its reliability assurances.

</details>

### [30] [Emergent Sparsity in Frozen Random CNN Feature Extractors for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26059)
*Scott M. Norton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度强化学习智能体在使用冻结的随机初始化CNN特征提取器时，会自发形成极度稀疏的全连接层表示（如Pong游戏中仅激活1-3个神经元），这种稀疏性随任务复杂度变化，且对智能体性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者偶然发现使用冻结随机CNN的智能体自发形成极稀疏表示，为理解深度强化学习中的信息压缩机制、随机特征表示的有效性以及神经网络内部表示结构提供了新的研究视角。

Method: 在多个Atari游戏（Pong、Breakout、Space Invaders）上训练PPO智能体，对比冻结随机CNN与可训练CNN的表现，通过神经元激活分析、消融实验、宽度缩放实验和时间演化分析等方法系统性研究稀疏现象。

Result: 1) FC1稀疏性随任务复杂度增加（Pong:1-11, Breakout:19-26, Space Invaders:~42）；2) 同游戏内不同随机种子产生不同稀疏度，影响最终性能上限；3) 消融活跃神经元导致性能崩溃，证明其必要性；4) 活跃神经元集合在奖励变正前15-105M步就已锁定；5) 冻结CNN与可训练CNN通过不同结构瓶颈达到竞争性奖励。

Conclusion: 当输入维度远超任务内在维度时，冻结随机投影上的梯度下降可在无显式稀疏机制下揭示问题的有效秩，为理解神经网络信息瓶颈机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: We report a striking phenomenon: deep reinforcement learning agents trained with frozen, randomly initialized CNN feature extractors spontaneously develop extremely sparse fully-connected representations, without any sparsity-inducing objective. In the first fully-connected layer (FC1, $3{,}136 \to 64$), agents compress task-relevant information through as few as 1-3 neurons out of 64 for deterministic Pong (5-11 for stochastic Pong), while trainable CNNs activate 55-64 neurons under matched conditions. We establish four principal findings. First, FC1 sparsity scales with task complexity: 1-11 for Pong, 19-26 for Breakout, and $\sim$42 for Space Invaders. Width-scaling confirms this reflects task structure rather than a fixed capacity fraction. Second, within-game scaling emerges: three identical Pong seeds produce 5, 7, and 11 active neurons. The 5-neuron seed plateaus at $+14$ reward, while the others reach expert performance ($+18.4$, $+18.7$), suggesting the random projection's usable dimensionality bounds achievable performance. Third, ablation confirms necessity: removing these active neurons crashes performance across two PPO implementations and four games. Fourth, the information bottleneck commits early: a sweep shows the active set locks by 15-30M steps, while reward turns positive 35-105M steps later. A complementary finding in Breakout shows frozen and trainable CNNs reach competitive rewards via structurally different bottlenecks: frozen agents use 17-25 active neurons (participation ratio $\sim$10-14), while trainable agents use 51 (participation ratio $\sim$3.6). Finally, wherever input dimensionality dwarfs intrinsic task dimensionality, gradient descent on a frozen random projection may reveal the effective rank of the underlying problem without explicit sparsity machinery.

</details>


### [31] [Sim2Win: A Team-Agnostic, Event-Based Pre-Match Outcome Prediction and Tactical Profiling System for Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26061)
*Mouad Zemzoumi,Amine Abouaomar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sim2Win，一个团队无关的赛前战术推荐框架，通过行为特征而非身份特征实现了对未见团队的泛化，在跨比赛数据集上ROCAUC达到0.704，准确率55.4%，全面超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业足球赛前战术决策严重依赖主观专家分析和基于身份的球探系统，无法泛化到训练中未见过的球队，亟需一种团队无关、可泛化的战术决策支持方法。

Method: 使用StatsBomb开放事件数据（涵盖11项赛事、178支球队、1411条球队-比赛记录），构建五场比赛滚动战术特征，设计四个可解释的战术特征比率，通过K-Means将球队行为聚类为八种比赛风格，训练十三个分类器从战术对阵表示中预测胜平负概率。系统不使用球队名称或身份特征，并通过Leave-One-Competition-Out（LOCO）严格评估。

Result: 在完全未见的球队上，Sim2Win实现平均ROC-AUC为0.704、平均准确率55.4%，在全部21项ROC-AUC对比和21项准确率对比中的19项上超越ELO、Pi-Rating和GAP基线。CatBoost在分布内表现最强，准确率达60.90%。

Conclusion: 基于行为的战术表示在分布偏移下具有可迁移的预测信号，为依赖身份的足球预测系统提供了可行的替代方案，证明了团队无关方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Pre-match tactical decision-making in professional football relies heavily on subjective expert analysis and identity-based scouting systems that cannot generalize to unseen teams. This paper presents Sim2Win, a team-agnostic, event-based pre-match tactical recommendation framework that reframes match outcome prediction as a tactical decision-support problem. Using StatsBomb open event data from eleven competitions spanning 178 teams and 1,411 team-match records, Sim2Win constructs five-match rolling tactical profiles, engineers four interpretable tactical feature ratios, clusters team behaviors into eight playstyles via K-Means, and trains thirteen classifiers to estimate win, draw, and loss probabilities from tactical matchup representations.
  The system operates without team names or identity features, enabling generalization to teams never seen during training. A rigorous Leave-One-Competition-Out (LOCO) evaluation demonstrates that Sim2Win achieves a mean ROC-AUC of 0.704 and mean accuracy of 55.4% on completely unseen teams, outperforming ELO, Pi-Rating, and GAP baselines on all 21 ROC-AUC comparisons and 19 of 21 accuracy comparisons. Among all evaluated models, CatBoost achieved the strongest in-distribution performance with 60.90% accuracy.
  These findings suggest that behavioral tactical representations provide transferable predictive signal under distribution shift and offer a viable alternative to identity-dependent football prediction systems.

</details>


### [32] [Meta-Learned Reward Shaping for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26094)
*Yunpeng Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MeRLa框架，通过元学习任务感知的奖励塑形函数来增强RLHF，在LLaMA-3-8B上显著优于现有方法且训练更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF方法使用静态、任务无关的奖励模型，与具体任务不匹配，导致学习信号稀疏和对齐效果次优。

Method: 提出MeRLa框架，在RLHF训练前跨辅助任务元学习任务感知的塑形函数Φ(x,y;φ)，生成复合奖励。元目标结合任务判别、熵正则化和基于势能的保守性以保证稳定收敛。提供策略不变性的理论保证，分析表示漂移敏感性，并形式化解决熵最大化导致的激励不匹配问题。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B上的实验表明，MeRLa在四个基准上持续优于PPO、DPO、GRPO和DAPO，在AlpacaEval 2.0上达到90.8%的长度控制胜率，在MT-Bench上获得9.14分，训练不稳定性降低41%。与过程式和评分标准增强奖励结合时仍保持优势。

Conclusion: MeRLa通过任务感知的奖励塑形有效解决了RLHF中的任务不匹配问题，在保持策略最优性的同时提供任务特定的学习信号，实现了更好的对齐效果和训练稳定性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard approach for aligning large language models with human preferences, but its quality is limited by static, task-agnostic reward models. This mismatch leads to sparse learning signals and suboptimal alignment. We introduce MeRLa (Meta-Learned Reward Shaping), a principled framework that meta-learns a task-aware shaping function $Φ(x,y;φ)$ across auxiliary tasks before RLHF training. The learned shaping produces a composite reward that preserves policy optimality while providing task-specific learning signals. Our meta-objective combines task discrimination, entropy regularization, and potential-based conservation for stable convergence. We provide theoretical guarantees for policy invariance, analyze representation drift sensitivity, and formally address incentive misalignment from entropy maximization. Experiments on LLaMA-3-8B across four benchmarks show consistent improvements over PPO, DPO, GRPO, and DAPO, achieving a 90.8% length-controlled win rate on AlpacaEval 2.0 and a score of 9.14 on MT-Bench, with 41% less training instability. MeRLa retains its benefits when combined with process-based and rubric-based enhanced rewards.

</details>


### [33] [Data Fusion and Contrastive Alignment for Unconstrained IR Molecular Structure Elucidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26164)
*Ethan J. Mick,Campbell A. Sweet,Matthias J. Young,Derek T. Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的编码器-解码器Transformer模型，用于从红外光谱数据中自动推断分子结构，通过引入混合专家解码器和非加性聚合操作，将Top-K预测准确率提高了10个百分点以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的红外光谱分子结构自动推断方法依赖于预先给定的化学式作为辅助输入，限制了模型只能进行同分异构体识别而非完整的分子结构预测。尽管Transformer模型在同分异构体识别方面表现良好，但在无约束结构推断任务中的可靠性较低且缺乏深入理解。

Method: 提出了对传统编码器-解码器Transformer的关键改进：(1) 设计了新颖的混合专家(MoE)解码器模块，采用非加性聚合方法（线性顺序统计量和Choquet积分）；(2) 在聚合光谱表示时也使用这些非加性操作符；(3) 引入辅助对比对齐损失函数。

Result: 相比基线红外光谱模型，Top-K预测准确率提升了超过10个百分点。通过分子预测的子结构片段分析，证实红外光谱编码了绝大部分相关化学信息，表明同分异构体排序模型的较高性能主要源于所探索化学空间中分子的吸收带代表性不足或重叠。

Conclusion: 证明了从测量红外光谱进行自动分子结构推断的有效性，显著拓宽了人工智能在分析化学中的应用范围，为无约束分子结构预测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Automated molecular structure elucidation from infrared (IR) spectroscopy data has seen significant advancements in recent years, but its broad applicability is limited by a reliance on pre-determined chemical formulas provided as auxiliary model inputs. This limits model predictions to isomer identification rather than full molecular structure prediction. Although transformer models have been shown to identify molecular isomers with high accuracy, their reliability for unconstrained structure elucidation is comparatively low and poorly understood. In this work, we propose and evaluate key modifications to the traditional encoder-decoder transformer. To better address the vast chemical space of the unconstrained problem, we implement a novel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoder module that utilizes non-additive aggregation via linear-order statistics and the Choquet integral. We further modify the transformer to utilize these non-additive operators when aggregating spectral representations as well. Together with an auxiliary contrastive alignment loss term, these enhancements improve Top-K prediction accuracy by over 10 percentage points compared to baseline IR-only models. Through sub-structure fragment analysis of molecular predictions, we further confirm that infrared spectra encode the vast majority of relevant chemical information, implying that the higher performance of isomer-ranking models is largely due to underrepresented or overlapping absorption bands for molecules in the explored chemical space. Ultimately, by demonstrating the efficacy of automated molecular structure elucidation from measured IR spectra, this work serves to significantly broaden the utility of AI in analytical chemistry.

</details>


### [34] [Shared SFT Lessons Across Alignment, Model Organisms, and Toy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26173)
*Anton de la Fuente,Arthur Conmy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在不同研究领域间迁移监督微调（SFT）的经验教训。研究发现：基于原因的训练比基于示例的训练泛化性更好；混合良性数据可防止能力损伤；后续良性训练可能擦除对齐行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐训练、模型生物和玩具模型这三个研究领域通常被分开研究，但它们都使用监督微调（SFT）追求相似的目标。当项目共享目标时，应该检验从一个领域学到的经验是否可以迁移到其他领域，从而提升各领域的研究进展。

Method: 研究三个跨领域的经验迁移：1）将对齐训练中行为泛化的教训迁移到玩具模型；2）将模型生物中能力保持的教训迁移到Model-Spec Midtraining对齐设置；3）将模型生物中鲁棒性的教训迁移到相同的对齐设置。通过实验验证每个迁移的效果。

Result: 三个迁移实验的结果：1）基于行为原因的训练比仅基于行为示例的训练具有更好的泛化性；2）混合良性数据可防止离模型输出SFT造成的能力损伤，同时保留目标行为；3）后续的良性SFT可能擦除对齐行为但保留能力，表明能力保持不能确保对后续训练的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在不同SFT研究领域间迁移经验教训可以提升所有相关领域，建议更多研究者应该借鉴其他领域的技术方法，促进跨领域研究合作。

Abstract: Alignment training, model organisms, and toy models are usually treated as separate research areas. But projects in all three frequently use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to pursue the same underlying goals. When projects share a goal, we should test whether lessons learned from one area transfer to the other areas. We study three such transfers, each taking a lesson developed in one SFT setting and testing it in another. First, we port a lesson about behavior generalization from alignment training into toy models. Training on the reason for a behavior, as in Teaching Claude Why, can make the behavior generalize better than training on examples of the behavior alone. Second, we port a lesson about capability preservation from model organisms into the Model-Spec Midtraining alignment setting. SFT on outputs written by a model other than the student (off-model outputs) can damage capabilities when trained on. Mixing in benign on-model (and on-policy) data into our training can prevent most of this damage while still embedding the target behavior. Third, we port a lesson about robustness from model organisms into the same alignment setting. We find that follow-up benign SFT can erase the alignment behavior while preserving capabilities, showing that capability preservation alone does not ensure robustness to subsequent training. Our work illustrates how porting SFT lessons between different research fields can uplift them all, suggesting more researchers should borrow techniques from outside their own areas.

</details>
